SELECT [v].[VisitId], [v].[BranchId], [v].[CreatedAt], [v].[CurrentAssignmentId], [v].[Department], [v].[IsReferred], [v].[PatientId], [v].[PaymentCollectionModel], [v].[ReferralPartnerId], [v].[ReferrerId], [v].[ReferrerText], [v].[RowVersion], [v].[Status], [v].[Token], [v].[TokenDate], [p].[PatientId], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[CurrentPhoneNumber], [p].[DateOfBirth], [p].[DisplayName], [p].[FirstName], [p].[Gender], [p].[IsDateOfBirthKnown], [p].[IsSoftDeleted], [p].[LastName], [p].[MRN], [p].[RowVersion], [p].[UpdatedAt], [r].[ReferralPartnerId], [r].[ContactInfo], [r].[CreatedAt], [r].[IsActive], [r].[Name], [r].[PartnerType], [r].[PaymentCollectionModel], [r].[UpdatedAt], [t0].[OrderId], [t0].[CancellationReason], [t0].[CancelledAt], [t0].[CancelledByUserId], [t0].[CreatedAt], [t0].[Department], [t0].[Discount], [t0].[Price], [t0].[SpecimenId], [t0].[Status], [t0].[TestCode], [t0].[TestId], [t0].[VisitId], [t0].[TestId0], [t0].[Category], [t0].[CreatedAt0], [t0].[DepartmentId], [t0].[ExtraInfo], [t0].[IsActive], [t0].[IsProfile], [t0].[SpecialInstructions], [t0].[SpecimenTypeCode], [t0].[TAT_Hours], [t0].[TestCode0], [t0].[TestName], [t0].[UpdatedAt], [t1].[InvoiceId], [t1].[CreatedAt], [t1].[Currency], [t1].[DiscountAmount], [t1].[DueDate], [t1].[GrossAmount], [t1].[NetAmount], [t1].[Status], [t1].[TaxAmount], [t1].[Total], [t1].[VisitId], [t1].[PaymentId], [t1].[Amount], [t1].[InvoiceId0], [t1].[Method], [t1].[ReceiptNo], [t1].[ReceivedAt], [t1].[ReceivedByUserId]

INNER JOIN [Patients] AS [p] ON [v].[PatientId] = [p].[PatientId]
LEFT JOIN [ReferralPartners] AS [r] ON [v].[ReferralPartnerId] = [r].[ReferralPartnerId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [o].[OrderId], [o].[CancellationReason], [o].[CancelledAt], [o].[CancelledByUserId], [o].[CreatedAt], [o].[Department], [o].[Discount], [o].[Price], [o].[SpecimenId], [o].[Status], [o].[TestCode], [o].[TestId], [o].[VisitId], [t].[TestId] AS [TestId0], [t].[Category], [t].[CreatedAt] AS [CreatedAt0], [t].[DepartmentId], [t].[ExtraInfo], [t].[IsActive], [t].[IsProfile], [t].[SpecialInstructions], [t].[SpecimenTypeCode], [t].[TAT_Hours], [t].[TestCode] AS [TestCode0], [t].[TestName], [t].[UpdatedAt]
    FROM [Orders] AS [o]
    INNER JOIN [Tests] AS [t] ON [o].[TestId] = [t].[TestId]
) AS [t0] ON [v].[VisitId] = [t0].[VisitId]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [i].[InvoiceId], [i].[CreatedAt], [i].[Currency], [i].[DiscountAmount], [i].[DueDate], [i].[GrossAmount], [i].[NetAmount], [i].[Status], [i].[TaxAmount], [i].[Total], [i].[VisitId], [p0].[PaymentId], [p0].[Amount], [p0].[InvoiceId] AS [InvoiceId0], [p0].[Method], [p0].[ReceiptNo], [p0].[ReceivedAt], [p0].[ReceivedByUserId]
    FROM [Invoices] AS [i]
    LEFT JOIN [Payments] AS [p0] ON [i].[InvoiceId] = [p0].[InvoiceId]
) AS [t1] ON [v].[VisitId] = [t1].[VisitId]
